    文献研究：Cure State Estimation in Frontal Polymerization from Temperature Fields via Cascaded Inverse-Forward Deep Learning with Uncertainty Quantification#
    Cure State Estimation in Frontal Polymerization from Temperature Fields via Cascaded Inverse-Forward Deep Learning with Uncertainty Quantification#
    📊 含图深析
    作者：Ikram Arif、Grant Litynski、WaiChing Sun、Xiang Zhang。Preprints，2026-09-10
    🤖 AI×科学 · AI×化学·材料AI×科学交叉8方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    Frontal polymerization (FP) is an emerging thermoset curing strategy that enables rapid, energy-efficient manufacturing of polymers and polymer composites through self-propagating exothermic polymerization fronts. The degree of cure, the primary determinant of mechanical properties, cannot be measured directly during processing, but the accompanying temperature field carries strong, well-defined features (notably the sharp gradients across the front) that can be acquired with reasonable accuracy in real time via infrared (IR) thermography. To date this thermal signal has been used mainly to track front kinematics rather than the underlying cure state, a task complicated by uncertainty in the initial degree of cure and the convection coefficient at boundaries. We address this gap with a cascaded deep learning pipeline: an inverse Stage 1 estimates these unknown but bounded initial and boundary conditions from the early-time temperature history, and a causal recurrent forward Stage 2 predicts the evolving cure field in real time from the temperature field, conditioned on the Stage 1 estimates. Trained on a finite-element dataset spanning the operating envelope, the pipeline attains low degree-of-cure root-mean-square error (RMSE): only 1.5× above the matched Stage 2 oracle given ground-truth conditions. A four-phase uncertainty quantification pipeline yields calibrated prediction intervals spanning ~1% of the cure range with 91% marginal coverage on a held-out test split. End-to-end inference runs in ≈0.1 s per trajectory on a single graphics processing unit (GPU). The framework is a simulation-validated prototype toward real-time, IR-driven process monitoring and digital-twin deployment, with experimental validation reserved for future work.

    📊 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题如何仅利用红外热成像获得的温度场，在初始固化度和边界对流换热系数未知的情况下，实时反演前沿聚合过程中的固化度场？
    创新方法提出级联逆向—正向深度学习框架：第一阶段从早期温度历史中识别受边界约束的未知初始条件与对流换热系数；第二阶段将这些估计结果与实时温度场输入因果循环神经网络，连续预测演化中的固化度场；同时通过四阶段不确定性量化生成带校准的固化度预测区间，实现从温度观测到固化状态的可信反演。核心突破是把条件识别与状态预测串联起来，降低未知过程条件对温度反演固化度的影响。
    工作流程首先利用有限元模型覆盖实际工作范围，生成包含温度场、固化度场、初始条件和边界换热条件的训练数据；输入前沿聚合早期红外温度历史，由 Stage 1 逆向估计未知初始固化度与边界对流换热系数；随后将实时温度场及 Stage 1 输出送入 Stage 2 因果循环神经网络，按时间推进预测固化度场；最后通过四阶段不确定性量化计算预测区间和覆盖率，输出可用于实时监测的固化度场及其可信度。
    关键结果在留出测试集上，固化度预测 RMSE 约为已知真实初始条件和边界条件的预知条件模型的 1.5 倍；预测区间覆盖约 1% 的固化度范围，并达到 91% 的边际覆盖率，显示出较好的不确定性校准能力；单个 GPU 上端到端推理每条轨迹约需 0.1 秒。结果目前主要由有限元仿真验证，尚缺乏实验系统验证及与完整现有基准方法的比较。
    应用价值将红外温度场从传统的聚合前沿运动跟踪信号扩展为固化度场实时观测依据，可用于聚合物制造过程监测、固化质量评估、异常预警和数字孪生；快速推理与非接触式测温兼容，支持在线部署，并通过预测区间为过程控制提供风险和可信度信息。

    核心概览

    论文提出一种级联深度学习方法，仅利用红外热成像温度场实时估计前沿聚合中的固化度场，属于聚合物制造过程监测、逆问题与不确定性量化方向。

    方法要点

    • 构建级联深度学习流程：先逆向估计未知初始固化度和边界对流换热系数，再正向预测固化度场。
    • Stage 1 根据早期温度历史，估计有界但未知的初始条件与边界条件。
    • Stage 2 采用因果循环神经网络，根据温度场和 Stage 1 输出实时预测演化中的固化度场。
    • 使用覆盖工作范围的有限元数据集进行训练。
    • 采用四阶段不确定性量化流程，生成固化度预测区间。
    • 推理以温度场为输入，面向实时红外驱动的过程监测和数字孪生应用。

    关键结果

    • 固化度预测的 RMSE 仅为具有真实初始及边界条件的 Stage 2“预知条件”模型的约 1.5 倍。
    • 预测区间覆盖约 1% 的固化度范围。
    • 在留出测试集上达到 91% 的边际覆盖率，表明预测区间具有较好的校准性。
    • 端到端推理速度约为每条轨迹 0.1 秒，运行于单个 GPU。
    • 该框架目前是经仿真验证的原型；实验验证留待未来工作，实际实验条件下的有效性摘要未详述。

    创新性判断

    • 可能的主要新颖点：将“未知初始/边界条件识别”和“固化度场预测”串联为级联逆向—正向学习框架，降低了仅由温度场反演固化状态时的条件不确定性影响。
    • 应用层面的创新：将温度场从传统的前沿运动跟踪信号，拓展为固化度场实时估计的观测依据。
    • 方法层面的创新：在固化度场预测中同时引入因果循环预测和校准不确定性量化，而非只输出点预测。
    • 工程价值：推理速度和红外测温兼容性支持实时过程监测及数字孪生部署的可能性。
    • 不确定性说明：摘要未提供与具体现有方法、实验系统或基准模型的完整对比，因此无法仅据摘要确认其相对领域工作的绝对领先程度；目前成果也主要由有限元仿真验证支撑。

    研究问题在平均 Zr 掺杂浓度相同的 Ba(Zr,Ti)O₃ 中，Zr 原子的空间分布方式如何改变材料的铁电、介电等功能行为？核心是区分“掺杂了多少”与“掺杂在哪里、如何排列”对宏观响应的不同影响。

    • 研究对象为 Zr 替位的 BaTiO₃，即 Ba(Zr,Ti)O₃ 类钙钛矿材料。
    • 代理模型的输入、训练数据来源、具体算法及所预测的物理量，摘要未详述。

    • 摘要明确指出：该类材料的功能行为通常按平均 Zr 浓度讨论，而掺杂空间分布的影响尚未被充分纳入这一讨论框架。
    • 论文显然旨在评估或建模掺杂空间分布对材料行为的影响；但由于摘要在“beyo…”处截断，具体结果、定量性能及验证结论，摘要未详述。

    创新性判断

    • 可能的新颖点在于：将 Zr 掺杂的空间分布从被忽略或简化的因素，提升为可由环级代理模型描述的建模变量，而不只使用平均掺杂浓度。
    • 若该模型能够以较低计算成本重现不同分布对应的性能变化，其贡献可能在于连接微观掺杂构型与宏观功能响应。
    • 上述判断仅基于题给的论文题目和不完整摘要，模型的具体创新、相对已有方法的优势及适用范围，摘要未详述。

    pyeCE：嵌入式团簇展开的Python实现#
    pyeCE: A Python Implementation of the Embedded Cluster Expansion#
    作者：Yann L. Müller、Claire A. Paetsch、Anirudh Raju Natarajan。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🤖 AI×科学 · AI×物理AI×科学交叉6方向匹配6

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：团簇展开是一种广泛使用的方法，可基于零开尔文第一性原理计算预测合金的有限温度热力学性质；但对于含有三种或四种以上化学元素的材料，其传统形式会变得难以处理。因此，高熵合金在很大程度上仍难以研究。我们提出pyeCE，这是一个开源Python库，实现了嵌入式团簇展开（eCE）；在该方法中，机器学习将许多化学元素映射到一组更小的有效元素，从而限制团簇函数数量的增长。pyeCE提供完整的建模工作流，包括构建具有可学习的按子晶格划分化学嵌入的对称性适配描述符、神经网络能量模型、阶梯式训练、不确定性量化，以及通过蒙特卡洛采样进行的有限温度模拟。该软件建立在PyTorch和pymatgen库之上，支持多子晶格上具有多种元素的体系，并可在图形处理器上运行。我们在两个材料体系上展示了该软件包。第一个案例中，一个覆盖九元难熔合金完整成分空间的单一模型解析了短程有序和有序-无序行为。该模型能够快速筛选具有强Cr团簇化的成分，而这一特征与连续耐腐蚀氧化物膜的形成相关。第二个案例中，Mo-Nb-W合金中氢溶解的模型再现了吸氢量对成分的依赖关系，并解析了氢所占据的间隙环境。pyeCE的模块化设计使其能够扩展至合金热力学以外的问题，包括动力学、缺陷能量学，以及化学有序与磁性和振动自由度之间的耦合。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations。

    📖 英文原文

    The cluster expansion is a widely used approach for predicting the finite-temperature thermodynamics of alloys from zero-kelvin first-principles calculations, but its conventional formulation becomes intractable for materials with more than three or four chemical species. High-entropy alloys have therefore remained largely out of reach. We present pyeCE, an open-source Python library that implements the embedded cluster expansion (eCE), in which machine learning maps many chemical species onto a smaller set of effective species and thereby limits the growth in the number of cluster functions. pyeCE provides the complete modeling workflow, including the construction of symmetry-adapted descriptors with a learnable per-sublattice chemical embedding, a neural-network energy model, ladder-based training, uncertainty quantification, and finite-temperature simulations through Monte Carlo sampling. Built on the PyTorch and pymatgen libraries, it supports systems with multiple species on multiple sublattices and runs on graphics processing units. We demonstrate the package on two material systems. In the first, a single model spanning the full composition space of a 9-component refractory alloy resolves short-range order and order--disorder behavior. This model enables rapid screening for compositions with strong Cr clustering, a feature linked to the formation of a continuous, corrosion-resistant oxide scale. In the second, a model of hydrogen dissolution in a Mo--Nb--W alloy reproduces the composition dependence of hydrogen uptake and resolves the interstitial environments that hydrogen occupies. The modular design of pyeCE allows it to be extended to problems beyond alloy thermodynamics, including kinetics, defect energetics, and the coupling of chemical order to magnetic and vibrational degrees of freedom.

    文献研究：The Structure of Emulations in Classical Spin Models: Modularity and Universality#
    The Structure of Emulations in Classical Spin Models: Modularity and Universality#
    📊 含图深析
    作者：Tobias Reinhart、Benjamin Engel、Gemma De les Coves。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🤖 AI×科学 · AI×物理AI×科学交叉6方向匹配3
    📖 英文原文

    The theory of spin models intersects with condensed matter physics, complex systems, graph theory, combinatorial optimization, computational complexity and neural networks. Many ensuing applications rely on the fact that complicated spin models can be transformed to simpler ones. What is the structure of such transformations? Here, we provide a framework to study and construct emulations between spin models. A spin model is a set of spin systems, and emulations are efficiently computable simulations with arbitrary energy cut-off, where a source spin system simulates a target system if, below the cut-off, the target Hamiltonian is encoded in the source Hamiltonian. We prove that emulations preserve important properties, as they induce reductions between computational problems such as computing ground states, approximating partition functions and approximate sampling from Boltzmann distributions. Emulations are modular (they can be added, scaled and composed), and allow for universality, i.e. certain spin models have maximal reach. We prove that a spin model is universal if and only if it is scalable, closed and functional complete. Because the characterization is constructive, it provides a step-by-step guide to construct emulations. We prove that the 2d Ising model with fields is universal, for which we also provide two new crossing gadgets. Finally, we show that simulations can be computed by linear programs. While some ideas of this work are contained in [1], we provide new definitions and theorems. This framework provides a toolbox for applications involving emulations of spin models.

    📊 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题如何用一种统一、严格且可组合的变换，描述复杂自旋模型由简单模型高效仿真，同时保留低能谱、基态、配分函数和玻尔兹曼采样等性质，并避免仅保持基态的变换过弱或图同构、图 minor 变换过强的问题？
    创新方法提出“simulation—emulation”统一框架：用能量截断 Δ、能量平移 Γ、局部物理自旋编码、解码映射和恒定简并度 d，使源系统在低能窗口内严格重现目标哈密顿量；进一步证明普适性的充要条件是 scalable、closed 和 functional complete，并以 crossing gadgets 与线性规划实现可构造仿真。
    工作流程输入目标自旋系统，先将其分解为局部相互作用对应的 canonical 或 flag spin systems 线性组合；再由具备 functional completeness 的模型模拟各个基本局部块，通过局部编码将目标自旋映射到互不重叠的物理自旋，利用解码恢复目标配置；最后借助 closure、scalability 和 modularity 合并、缩放各模块，输出可高效计算且支持任意能量截断的 emulation。
    关键结果证明 emulation 可保持基态能量、低能谱、配分函数近似和玻尔兹曼分布近似采样，并且仿真支持相加、缩放和复合；建立普适性的 scalable + closed + functional complete 充要刻画；证明带场二维 Ising 模型具有普适性，提出两种新的 crossing gadgets，并给出用线性规划求取 simulation 的方法。
    应用价值为凝聚态物理、组合优化、计算复杂性、图论和神经网络中的自旋模型变换提供统一工具，使研究者能够从局部 gadget 系统化构造任意目标模型的仿真，并在模型替换后继续分析基态、热力学性质和采样问题，支持用结构更简单或易实现的模型表达复杂计算任务。

    以下仅基于已提供文本；后续未给出的章节、定理证明与数值细节不扩展推断。

    第一部分：核心概览

    建立了经典自旋模型仿真/仿真族 emulation的统一数学框架，把“复杂自旋系统可由更简单模型替代”精确定义为带任意能量截断、局部编码、能量平移和常数简并的高效模拟。核心贡献在于证明该结构同时满足性质保持、模块化与普适性：可保持基态、谱、配分函数近似与玻尔兹曼采样问题；可组合、缩放、相加；并给出普适性的充要条件：scalable、closed、functional complete。该框架把凝聚态、组合优化、复杂性理论和神经网络中的自旋模型变换统一到同一语言中。

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《The Structure of Emulations in Classical Spin Models: Modularity and Universality》（None，）

    背景：自旋模型连接凝聚态物理、复杂系统、图论、组合优化、计算复杂性、神经网络。既有“变换”概念要么过弱，只保持基态问题；要么过强，如图同构或图 minor，只允许有限结构变化。

    方法：定义spin system为带局部相互作用的超图，定义simulation为源系统在能量截断 Δ 以下通过局部编码重现实目标哈密顿量，再定义emulation为可高效计算、可任意设定截断的 simulation 族。

    结果：emulation 保持多个计算问题结构，包括ground state energy、partition function approximation、approximate Boltzmann sampling；simulation/emulation 可compose、scale、add；普适性等价于scalability + closure + functional completeness。

    讨论：框架提供了构造任意目标自旋系统模拟的步骤：先把目标分解为flag spin systems的线性组合，再用 functional completeness 模拟基本块，最后用 closure 与 scalability 合成为整体。

    局限：已提供文本只展示定义、动机、部分例子和后续结果概述；完整证明、线性规划构造、2d Ising crossing gadgets 的具体结构未完整呈现。

    结论：该框架把自旋模型变换提升为可证明、可组合、可构造的理论工具，并证明带场二维 Ising 模型具有普适性，另给出新 crossing gadgets 与线性规划求 simulation 的方法。

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析

    Abstract

    Unified framework for emulations

    核心对象是spin model与emulation。spin model 被定义为一组 spin systems；emulation 是“可高效计算的 simulation 族”，并允许任意能量截断。摘要直接给出定义骨架：*"A spin model is a set of spin systems, and emulations are efficiently computable families of simulations with arbitrary energy cut-off"*。simulation 的物理含义是：在截断能量以下，源哈密顿量通过局部编码重现实目标哈密顿量，即 *"below the cut-off, the target Hamiltonian is encoded in the source Hamiltonian"*。

    Property preservation across computational tasks

    emulation 不只是形式映射，而是诱导多个计算问题之间的归约，包括基态计算、配分函数近似、玻尔兹曼分布近似采样。摘要给出问题清单：*"computing ground states, approximating partition functions and approximate sampling from Boltzmann distributions"*。这使 emulation 成为跨物理与复杂性理论的性质保持变换，而不是单纯图结构变换。

    Modularity and universality

    emulation 具备模块化，可相加、缩放、复合：*"Emulations are modular (they can be added, scaled and composed)"*。同时允许普适性，即某些自旋模型在 emulation 下具有最大可达范围：*"under emulations, certain spin models have maximal reach"*。

    Exact characterization of universality

    普适性的核心判据是充要条件：*"a spin model is universal if and only if it is scalable, closed and functional complete"*。该判据不仅是抽象定理，还具有构造性：*"Because the characterization is constructive, it provides a step-by-step guide to construct emulations"*。

    Concrete application to 2d Ising with fields

    带场二维 Ising 模型被证明为 universal，并提供两个新的 crossing gadgets：*"We prove that the 2d Ising model with fields is universal, for which we also provide two new crossing gadgets"*。simulation 还可通过线性规划计算：*"simulations can be computed by linear programs"*。

    I Introduction

    Broad disciplinary reach of spin models

    自旋模型最初是磁性的 toy models，但已扩展到多个学科。引言列出具体交叉领域：*"condensed matter physics, complex systems, graph theory, combinatorial optimization, computational complexity and neural networks"*。还通过 lattice gauge theories 连接高能物理，通过 Hopfield networks 与 Boltzmann machines 连接神经网络。

    Computational complexity as motivation

    Barahona 证明带场二维 Ising 模型的 NP-hardness 后，Ising ground state energy problem 成为组合优化核心对象。引言明确写道：*"Barahona’s proof of the NP hardness of the 2d Ising with fields"*，并指出这意味着 NP-complete problems 可变换为 Ising 模型的基态能量问题：*"NP-complete problems can be transformed to the ground state energy problem of the Ising model"*。

    Existing transformation notions are context-dependent

    不同领域对“变换”的要求不同。凝聚态关注相变性质和 dualities，如 Kramers-Wannier duality；复杂性理论关注 ground state energy reductions；图论可能关注 relabeling、graph minors；神经网络关注 Boltzmann distributions。核心问题被直接表述为：*"What is the structure of such transformations?"*

    Weak transformations fail thermodynamic preservation

    只保持基态的归约过弱，因为高能级可能失真，无法恢复配分函数。引言指出：*"These transformations, however, need not behave well on high energy levels"*，因此 *"it may not be possible to recover the partition function of the target from that of the source"*。

    Strong graph-like transformations unnecessarily restrict reach

    图同构或 graph minor 型变换过强，会保留太多图性质，从而限制模型可达范围。例子是 planar graphs：*"spin models on planar graphs could only solve optimization problems with planar connectivity"*。这与 universal spin models 的目标冲突。

    Desired transformation criteria

    理想变换需满足三点：property-preserving、universality、modularity。引言给出总结：*"we want a notion of transformation between spin models that (i) is property-preserving for all contexts, (ii) allows for universality, and (iii) is modular"*。

    Simulation definition by energy cut-off and local encoding

    如果源系统 S simulates 目标系统 T，则在能量截断 Δ 以下，S 与 T 的哈密顿量通过局部编码一致，允许整体能量平移 Γ。原句为：*"below an energy cut-off Δ the Hamiltonians of S and T agree up to a global energy shift Γ and a local encoding of configurations of T into configurations of S"*。

    Constructive universality through three properties

    普适性通过closure、scalability、functional completeness刻画。图 2 的逻辑是：目标系统分解为 flag spin systems 的线性组合；每个基本块由 universal model 模拟；线性组合再由 closure 与 scalability 模拟；模块化保证组合后得到目标 simulation。引言原句：*"The characterization is constructive and thus provides the means to explicitly emulate arbitrary targets"*。

    New contributions beyond De16b

    相对 De16b，新增点包括新 simulation 定义、谱和基态保持、partition function 与 Boltzmann distribution 近似保持、isomorphisms/weak symmetries/spin type modifications/graph minors 诱导 simulation、simulation 模块化、emulation 组合与 hull lifting、普适性新刻画、复杂性归约、2d Ising 新证明、线性规划求 simulation。引言明确列出：*"This work focuses on the mathematical structure of simulations and emulations"*，并强调 *"new definitions and theorems"*。

    II Spin Systems and Simulations

    Chapter scope

    该章建立底层对象：spin systems、simulations及其modularity。章节说明为：*"Here we define spin systems (Section II.1), simulations (Section II.2), and prove that the latter are modular (Section II.3)"*。

    II.1 Spin Systems

    Spin system as hyperedge-labelled hypergraph

    spin system 是四元组 (S=(q_S,V_S,E_S,J_S))，其中 (q_Sge 2)，(V_S) 是有限自旋集合，(E_Ssubseteq mathcal P(V_S)) 是超边集合，(J_S(e):[q_S]^e→ mathbb Rgₑ₀) 是局部相互作用。直观解释是：*"a spin system is a hyperedge-labelled hypergraph"*，其中 hyperedges 决定哪些 spins interact，labels 决定 interaction form。

    No isolated spins assumption

    要求没有孤立自旋：每个 (vin V_S) 至少属于一个 (ein E_S)。若存在孤立点，可加零函数超边消除，且不改变局部相互作用。原文说明：*"We require that there are no isolated spins"*，并指出可通过 *"adding any such edge e with J_S(e) the zero function"* 修正。

    Hamiltonian as sum of local energies

    给定配置 (vecsin mathcal C_S=[q_S]V_S)，每条超边产生局部能量 (J_S(e)(vecs|ₑ))，哈密顿量为总和：

    [

    H_S(vecs)=sumₑᵢₙ E_SJ_S(e)(vecs|ₑ).

    ]

    定义句为：*"Adding these local energies yields a global energy (or cost) function, namely the Hamiltonian of the spin system"*。

    Non-negative local interactions are without loss of generality

    局部相互作用限制为非负函数，但不损失一般性，因为任意局部相互作用可通过整体平移变为非负。原文解释：*"This definition is equivalent to that of arbitrary local interactions, as a spin system with arbitrary local interactions can be transformed to have non-negative local interactions by shifting all local interactions"*。该过程还保持多项式时间可计算。

    Ising system as canonical example

    Ising 系统使用 (q=2) 的二能级 spins，pair interactions 只依赖两个 spin 的 parity。两类相互作用为 (π₂) 与 (barπ₂)：前者在 (sᵢ=sⱼ) 时为 1，后者在 (sᵢne sⱼ) 时为 1。原文表述为：*"Up to scaling (and relabeling the spins) there are two such interactions"*。

    Fields as single-spin interactions

    带场 Ising 系统加入单自旋超边 (i)，并定义 (π₁) 与 (barπ₁)。原文为：*"Additionally, one might include single-spin hyperedges i in the edge set of the interaction graph"*，这样得到 *"Ising spin system with fields"*。

    System size as parameter count

    spin system 大小定义为

    [

    |S|=sumₑᵢₙ E_Sq_S|e|.

    ]

    这是描述所有局部函数所需实参数量。原文：*"we define the size of a spin system S as the length of this description, i.e. the number of real valued parameters that are required to specify it"*。若 spin type 常数且相互作用 arity 有上界 (k)，则 (|S|=poly(|V_S|))。

    Local decomposition is structurally essential

    每个 spin system 不只是全局 cost function，也编码局部项如何组成整体。原文强调：*"Each spin system not only gives rise to a global cost function but also encodes how this global function is composed of local terms"*。这对 simulation 的模块化至关重要，因为复杂系统可分解成简单局部项再组合。

    Frustration illustrates local-global mismatch

    局部最小化不必导出全局最小化，原因是frustration：局部最优子配置在重叠处可能不一致。原文定义为：*"if the sub-configurations that locally minimize H_S do not agree on the overlaps of local terms"*。这解释了为何 simulation 需兼容 sum，而不能只处理全局函数。

    Sum of spin systems

    两个同 spin type 的系统 (S₁,S₂) 的和定义在 (V₁cup V₂)、(E₁cup E₂) 上，重叠超边的相互作用相加。关键等式为：

    [

    HS_₁₊S_₂(vecs)=HS_₁(vecs|_VS_₁)+HS_₂(vecs|_VS_₂).

    ]

    原文说明：*"By construction, the sum of spin systems is both associative and commutative"*。

    Scaling of spin systems

    非负实数 (λ) 缩放系统为 (λ· S)，并满足

    [

    Hλ· S(vecs)=λ H_S(vecs).

    ]

    物理解释为温度变化：*"In thermodynamic terms, scaling a system by a positive constant can be understood as changing its temperature"*。负缩放被排除，因为会交换高能与低能结构。

    Canonical spin system

    任意局部函数 (f:[q]^e→mathbb Rgₑ₀) 可定义单超边 canonical spin system (S_f)，并满足 (HS_f=f)。任意系统可分解为 canonical systems 之和：

    [

    S=sumₑᵢₙ E_SSJ_S₍ₑ₎.

    ]

    原文强调：*"every spin system S is trivially the sum of the canonical systems corresponding to its local interactions"*。

    Spin system isomorphism

    isomorphism 是 spin labels 的双射 (φ:V_S→ V_T)，保持超边和局部相互作用，并满足哈密顿量按 relabeling 一致：

    [

    H_S(vecs)=H_T(vecscircφ⁻¹).

    ]

    原文总结：*"Isomorphic spin systems are equivalent in all aspects"*。

    II.2 Simulations

    Simulation weakens isomorphism while preserving relevant low-energy structure

    isomorphism 过强，因为很多系统虽非同构，但在局部编码或低能区间内等价。章节动机为：*"two spin systems S and T can be considered at least approximately equivalent in all relevant aspects, even though they are not isomorphic"*。

    Three preservation targets

    simulation 后续将保持谱、基态、热力学性质。章节说明列出：*"preserve spectra and ground states"*，以及 *"thermodynamic properties"*。这比只保持 ground state energy 更强。

    II.2.1 Definition

    Low-energy Hamiltonian matching

    simulation 的核心等式是

    [

    (H_S(vecs)-Γ)|<Δ=H_T(deccirc vecscirc P)|<Δ.

    ]

    含义是：源配置经 physical spin assignment (P) 与 decoding (dec) 得到目标配置，在 cut-off (Δ) 以下能量完全匹配，允许整体 shift (Γ)。原文解释：*"P and dec describe how low energy configurations s of S are mapped to low energy configurations ... of T with equal energy up to Γ"*。

    Simulation data

    simulation (mathcal f:S→ T) 包含六类数据：cut-off (Δ>0)、energy shift (Γinmathbb R)、degeneracy (dinmathbb N)、physical spin assignment (P:V_T→ V_S^k)、decoding (dec:[q_S]^k→[q_T])、encoding list ((encᵢ:[q_T]→[q_S]^k)ᵢ₌₁,ₗdₒₜₛ,ₘ)。原文称这些为：*"cut-off"*, *"energy shift"*, *"degeneracy"*, *"physical spin assignment"*, *"decoding"*, *"encoding"*。

    Disjoint physical spins

    不同目标 spins 的 physical representatives 必须互不重叠：

    [

    P⁽ⁱ⁾(v)=P⁽j⁾(w)Rightarrow i=j and v=w.

    ]

    该条件保证局部解码不会混淆目标自由度，原文命名为 *"Disjoint physical spins"*。

    Decode-encode compatibility

    每个 encoding 必须被 decoding 还原：

    [

    deccirc encᵢ=id.

    ]

    原文命名为 *"Decode-encode compatibility"*。这避免不同目标 spin values 在源空间中不可区分。

    Disjoint encodings

    同一目标值 (t) 在不同 encoding branch 中不能相同，除非 branch index 相同：

    [

    encᵢ(t)=encⱼ(t)Rightarrow i=j.

    ]

    原文命名为 *"Disjoint encodings"*。该条件控制 encoding branches 的简并结构。

    Constant local degeneracy

    对每个低能目标配置 (vec t)，每个 encoding branch (i) 对应的 source configurations 数量必须恒为 (d)：

    [

    H_T(vec t)<ΔRightarrow |simᵢ(vec t)|=d.

    ]

    原文命名为 *"Constant local degeneracy"*。这是配分函数与玻尔兹曼分布近似保持的关键条件。

    Matching energies and high-energy exclusion

    若 (vecsinsim(vec t))，则

    [

    H_S(vec s)-Γ=H_T(vec t).

    ]

    若 (vecs) 不模拟任何目标配置，则

    [

    H_S(vec s)-ΓgeΔ.

    ]

    原文命名为 *"Matching energies"*，确保 cut-off 以下没有多余低能源态污染目标谱。

    Local recoverability of target configurations

    若 (vec s) 模拟 (vec t)，则目标 spin (v) 的状态可由源系统中 (P(v)) 的 physical spins 局部恢复：

    [

    vec t(v)=deccirc vec scirc P(v).

    ]

    原文强调：*"This means that t can be recovered from s locally"*。

    Auxiliary spins may vary

    source 中未被 (P) 指派的 spins 是 auxiliary spins；即使目标配置只改一个 spin，source 配置也可能在 auxiliary spins 上不同。原文：*"The remaining spins of S are called auxiliary spins"*，并指出 *"s and s' can always differ on auxiliary spins"*。

    Need for both decoding and encoding

    只用 encoding 会导致低能 source 配置无法唯一 decoding；只用 decoding 又无法保证目标局部配置可局部编码。原文给出原因：*"The decoding ensures that the individual encodings are compatible"*，并进一步说明若无 encoding，simulation 将 *"be incompatible with sums (and hence decompositions) of spin systems"*。

    Example 9: explicit finite simulation into an Ising source

    目标系统 (T) 有 (q_T=3)、(V_T=1,2,3)、超边 (1,2,3,1,2)。局部相互作用给出三体项与二体项，cut-off (Δ=3)。低于 3 的目标配置恰有 4 个：((1,1,1),(2,2,2),(3,3,3),(1,2,3))，其中前三个能量为 1，第四个能量为 2。原文写明：*"There are exactly 4 target configurations with energy below 3"*，并给出 *"H_T(t₁)=H_T(t₂)=H_T(t₃)=1, H_T(t₄)=2"*。

    Example 9: source Ising system and encoding order

    source 是带场 Ising 系统，(q_S=2)，(V_S=1,ldots,10)，energy shift (Γ=3)，cut-off (Δ=3)。每个目标 spin 被编码为一对 source spins：(P(a)=(1,2))、(P(b)=(3,4))、(P(c)=(5,6))。图注总结为：*"Each spin of T is encoded into a pair of spins of S"*。

    Example 9: spectral agreement below cut-off

    图 4(b) 显示 (H_S-Γ) 与 (H_T) 在 (Δ=3) 以下谱相同，且每个低能目标配置对应唯一 source 配置。原文说明：*"Below the cut-off, the spectra of H_S−Γ and H_T are identical"*，并补充 *"Above the cut-off, the two spectra are only drawn schematically"*。

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持

    Emulation

    不是单个 simulation，而是高效可计算的 simulation 族，并且支持任意 cut-off。语义比 “simulation” 更强，因为 emulation 是模型层级的可构造变换：*"efficiently computable families of simulations with arbitrary energy cut-off"*。

    Reach

    指一个 spin model 通过允许的变换能到达的所有目标模型集合。*"Universal spin models thus have maximal reach"* 中的 reach 不是物理空间范围，而是表达能力/可模拟范围。

    Functional completeness

    来自逻辑与约束满足语境，表示模型能实现足够丰富的局部函数基，从而模拟任意局部 interaction。这里与 closure、scalability 结合，构成 universal 的充要条件。

    Cut-off

    (Δ) 不是近似误差，而是能量窗口边界。simulation 要求 (Δ) 以下严格匹配，(Δ) 以上可不匹配。句子 *"below the cut-off, the target Hamiltonian is encoded in the source Hamiltonian"* 的重点是低能谱嵌入，而非全谱等价。

    Degeneracy

    (d) 表示每个目标低能配置在每个 encoding branch 下对应的 source 配置数量恒定。若简并不恒定，配分函数和 Boltzmann 权重会被重新加权，热力学性质无法保持。

    复杂概念：为什么 constant local degeneracy 必要

    配分函数是 (sumᵥₑc ₛe⁻β H_S⁽vec s⁾)。若目标配置 (vec t₁,vec t₂) 的 source preimages 数量不同，即使能量匹配，Boltzmann 概率也会多出不同 multiplicity 因子，导致分布扭曲。constant local degeneracy 让所有低能目标配置只获得同一倍数 (m· d)，归一化后概率比例保持不变。

    复杂概念：为什么 simulation 必须局部编码

    若目标 spin 的值只能从 source 全局配置推断，多个局部 simulation 无法自然相加。局部编码保证每个目标局部项可独立替换成 source gadget，随后通过 sum 组合为整体系统。这正是 modularity 的技术基础。

    复杂概念：为什么 graph minor 变换太强

    graph minor 保留许多图结构性质，适合图论但不适合普适仿真。若限制在 planar connectivity，许多非平面问题无法嵌入；crossing gadget 的作用正是用局部能量结构模拟“边交叉”，在不破坏平面结构的情况下实现非平面相互作用。

    第五部分：创新评估与关联

    新颖性判断

    核心新颖性在于把过去分散在复杂性归约、Ising gadget、图变换、Boltzmann machine 表达能力中的“模拟”统一为一个严格结构：simulation → emulation → modularity → universality。相对于 De16b，新增的不只是例子，而是完整的定义系统、保持性定理、模块化定理和普适性充要刻画。

    解决的前人缺口

    前人常能证明某个模型对某个任务 hard 或 universal，但缺少一个同时解释以下问题的统一答案：哪些变换保持基态、谱、配分函数和采样？哪些变换可组合？怎样从局部 gadget 系统化构造全局 simulation？普适性的必要且充分结构条件是什么？这里给出的答案是：scalable、closed、functional complete 三者合取。

    相对近年工作的推进

    相对 Ko18 一类沿用 De16b 思路的 universality 结果，推进点是更精确的 simulation 定义与模块化证明；相对 Go23 的抽象范畴化方向，推进点是给出具体可计算的自旋模型工具箱；相对常见 NP-hardness reductions，推进点是保留热力学量和采样问题，而不只是 ground state energy。

    最强科学创新点

    普适性被转化为可检查、可构造的代数式结构条件：模型只要能缩放、能闭合地组合局部模拟、能函数完备地产生基本局部相互作用，就能 emulate 所有 spin systems。这使“universal spin model”从经验性 gadget 构造上升为结构定理。

    页内定位
  8. 13
    PatternFormer：学习反应-扩散系统中的多重解模式#
    PatternFormer: Learning Multiple Solution Patterns in Reaction--Diffusion Systems#
    📊 含图深析
    作者：Wenrui Hao、Zhipeng Chang、Wenpeng Yin。Preprints，2026-09-10
    🤖 AI×科学 · AI×物理AI×科学交叉6方向匹配3

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：物理、化学和生物学中的许多非线性模型在相同参数下呈现多个解，而捕获这一完整解集对于理解模式形成系统至关重要。然而，现有的学习代理模型在根本上是单值的：神经算子将每个参数映射为单一输出，而物理信息神经网络则收敛到一个分支。我们开发了PatternFormer（PF），这是一个基于大语言模型、用于学习非线性偏微分方程多重解的框架。通过将无序的共存解转换为规范序列，PF能够在单次自回归过程中生成结构化解集；对于有限解族可自动终止，而对于无界解族则施加物理残差约束。在非线性椭圆问题上，它能够在一次推理步骤中恢复所有解分支；在Gray-Scott系统上，它生成共存的图灵模式，其中包括参考数据中不存在且超出训练范围、但在物理上有效的状态。PF还可以在多稳态系统之间进行顺序微调，从而朝着用于解景观的通用基础模型发展。

    📖 英文原文

    Many nonlinear models across physics, chemistry, and biology exhibit multiple solutions for the same parameters, and capturing this entire solution set is essential for understanding pattern-forming systems. Yet existing learned surrogates are fundamentally single-valued: neural operators map each parameter to a single output, and physics-informed neural networks converge to one branch. We develop PatternFormer (PF), a large language model-based framework for learning the multiple solutions of nonlinear partial differential equations. By transforming unordered coexisting solutions into canonical sequences, PF produces structured solution sets in a single autoregressive pass, terminating automatically for finite families and enforcing physical residual constraints for unbounded ones. On nonlinear elliptic problems it recovers all solution branches in one inference step; on Gray--Scott it generates coexisting Turing patterns, including physically valid states absent from the reference data and beyond training. PF can also be sequentially fine-tuned across multistable systems, toward general foundation models for solution landscapes.

    📊 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题在同一组参数下，如何让模型同时学习非线性偏微分方程的多组共存解、不同解分支及其复杂模式景观，避免传统方法只收敛到单一解？
    创新方法提出 PatternFormer，将无序的多解集合规范化为可生成的序列，并利用大语言模型的自回归机制在一次推理中连续生成多个解分支；有限解族通过自动终止判断生成结束，无界解族则加入物理残差约束，确保新生成状态满足方程物理规律。
    工作流程输入非线性偏微分方程、系统参数与训练解数据；先将共存解排序并编码为规范化序列；模型逐步自回归生成解集合；对有限解族触发自动终止，对无界解族持续施加物理残差约束；输出完整解分支集合及对应的空间模式，可进一步在不同多稳态系统间顺序微调。
    关键结果在非线性椭圆问题中，模型能够一次推理恢复全部解分支；在 Gray–Scott 反应-扩散系统中成功生成共存的 Turing 模式；同时发现部分参考数据中不存在、但仍满足物理约束的有效状态，显示出一定的解空间补全与超训练范围外推能力。
    应用价值为多稳态反应-扩散系统和非线性 PDE 提供统一的多解生成工具，可快速探索完整解景观、发现隐藏稳态与新型空间模式，并为跨系统构建面向解景观的科学基础模型、辅助材料设计和复杂动力系统分析提供基础。

    核心概览

    论文提出基于大语言模型的 PatternFormer，用于学习非线性偏微分方程在同一参数下的多组共存解及其模式景观，属于科学机器学习与多稳态系统建模方向。

    方法要点

    • 将无序的共存解转换为规范化序列，使模型能够结构化生成解集合。
    • 采用自回归方式，在一次推理过程中生成多个解。
    • 对有限解族，通过自动终止机制结束生成。
    • 对无界解族，引入物理残差约束以维持物理一致性。
    • 使用大语言模型框架学习非线性偏微分方程的多分支解。
    • 在多稳态系统之间进行顺序微调，探索构建面向解景观的基础模型。

    关键结果

    • 在非线性椭圆问题上，模型能够在一次推理中恢复全部解分支。
    • 在 Gray–Scott 系统上，模型能够生成共存的 Turing 模式。
    • 模型生成了一些参考数据中不存在、且超出训练范围的物理有效状态。
    • 摘要声称该模型可跨多稳态系统顺序微调，但具体泛化性能和适用范围摘要未详述。

    创新性判断

    • 主要新颖点可能在于：将多解 PDE 学习转化为规范化序列生成问题，从而突破传统神经算子单值映射和 PINN 易收敛到单一分支的限制。
    • “一次自回归推理生成完整解集合”是区别于逐分支求解或重复采样方法的潜在贡献；但生成效率、完整性及与现有多解方法的定量比较，摘要未详述。
    • 将自动终止机制与物理残差约束分别用于有限和无界解族，体现了对不同解集合结构的适配；具体约束形式及理论保证摘要未详述。
    • 生成参考数据之外的物理有效模式，提示其可能具备一定外推或补全能力；但这些状态的验证标准、外推范围和可靠性仍无法仅凭摘要确定。
    • 跨多稳态系统顺序微调并迈向“解景观基础模型”具有扩展性意义，但目前更适合视为摘要提出的研究方向，实际通用性尚不能确定。

    文献研究：Surrogate-Assisted Inverse Design and Temperature-Dependent Electrothermal Analysis of an All-Oxide Narrowband Thermophotovoltaic Emitter#
    Surrogate-Assisted Inverse Design and Temperature-Dependent Electrothermal Analysis of an All-Oxide Narrowband Thermophotovoltaic Emitter#
    作者：Bibekananda Nath、Kawshik Nath、Ahmed Zubair。arXiv · physics，2026-09-10
    🤖 AI×科学 · AI×化学·材料AI×科学交叉6方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    A narrowband emitter aligning with the bandgap of the underlying solar cell is essential for improving the spectral efficiency and thermal stability of thermophotovoltaic (TPV) systems. Emitters based on oxide materials present a promising solution to the optical and mechanical performance degradation of traditional emitters, which employ metal-dielectric structures that experience metal oxidation and structural deterioration at high temperatures. Here, we presented a surrogate-assisted inverse-design framework for a narrowband 1D grating emitter comprising ITO and Al2O3 layers on a sapphire substrate. We performed Bayesian optimization over the trained ExtraTrees surrogates on a penalty-augmented objective containing a peak-emission constraint (E_peak > 0.90) while minimizing the full width at half maximum (FWHM) and maximizing the fraction of emission concentrated within the selected peak-centered spectral band to acquire a high, narrowband peak emission. The surrogate model was trained using a dataset of emission spectra obtained from the finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) by systematically varying the layers' thicknesses and the structure's period as the input features, and the narrowband emission's figure of merit (FOM) ((E_peak), wavelength of peak emission (_peak), FWHM, in-band fraction (f_in) for determining the amount of emission outside the peak band, and concentration of peak emission near the peak) was used as the prediction target. The resulting set of predictions for the optimized structure was further validated using the FDTD method.

    基于自旋空间群与图论的公度磁结构自动生成#
    Automated Generation of Commensurate Magnetic Structures based on Spin Space Groups and Graph Theory#
    作者：Yifan Wang、Boyang Deng、John Robertson、Weisheng Zhao、Stefan Blügel、Haichang Lu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5

    💡 导读：论文提出 SpinGraph 自动化流程，利用自旋空间群和图论生成满足对称性约束的、无重复的磁性结构。该方法可控制单重或多重波矢态的叠加，并与磁性分析程序结合提取自旋相互作用，论文还以单层 CrI₃ 为例进行了验证。

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：具有对称性约束的磁结构是可能在能量上更有利的构型候选。枚举这些结构对于识别实验观测至关重要，并且在提取自旋相互作用进行能量拟合时，可提供无偏、线性稳定且得到最优采样的参考构型。我们提出SpinGraph，这是一种用于生成对称性不同的磁构型的自动化工作流。SpinGraph不仅兼容更一般的自旋空间群，而且允许精确控制所指定的单Q态或多Q态叠加。对于有限群，我们直接枚举相容的子群。对于具有连续或特殊纯自旋部分的自旋空间群，首先枚举有限部分，然后将其与相容的精确纯自旋约束结合。这些操作被转换为实空间或傅里叶空间中的图约束。每个生成结构的对称性都会在构建后重新评估。最后，我们将SpinGraph与磁学分析代码AMATIS集成，对绝缘单层CrI3的自旋相互作用进行了全面计算。SpinGraph补充了AMATIS的最后一部分，实现了自旋哈密顿量构建的全自动工作流，这对于研究相变、自旋纹理、磁振子和自旋动力学至关重要。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与我们的磁性材料、非共线磁性、自旋哈密顿量、对称性分析、二维范德华磁体和图神经网络方向高度直接相关。自动生成对称性完备的磁结构可显著改进交换参数拟合、磁基态搜索和机器学习磁性模型的数据集构建。

    启发：建议重点阅读其自旋空间群枚举、连续自旋约束处理及与自旋哈密顿量拟合的接口。可进一步将该流程与主动学习、等变神经网络和高通量磁性材料筛选结合，研究自旋轨道耦合、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用及多重波矢磁序。

    📖 英文原文

    Magnetic structures with symmetry constraints are candidates for energetically favorable configurations. Enumerating these structures is essential for identifying experimental observations and provides unbiased, linearly stable, and optimally sampled reference configurations for energy fitting when extracting spin interactions. We present SpinGraph, an automated workflow generating symmetry-distinct magnetic configurations. SpinGraph is not only compatible with the more general spin space groups, but also allows precise control of the prescribed single- or multi-Q states superposition. For finite groups, we enumerate compatible subgroups directly. For spin space groups with a continuous or special spin-only part, the finite component is enumerated first and then combined with the compatible exact spin-only constraints. These actions are translated into graph constraints in real or Fourier space. The symmetry of every generated structure is re-evaluated after construction. Finally, we integrate SpinGraph with the magnetic analysis code AMATIS to perform a thorough calculation for the spin interactions of the insulating monolayer CrI3. SpinGraph complements the last part of AMATIS and realizes the fully automated workflow for the spin Hamiltonian construction, which is essential for studying phase transitions, spin textures, magnons, and spin dynamics.

    拓扑超导的第一性原理多尺度框架#
    A First-Principles Multiscale Framework for Topological Superconductivity#
    作者：Christopher L. Jacobs、Baishali Mandal、Taehwan Jung、Purna P. Paudel、Jason Kawasaki、Tudor D. Stanescu 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 拓扑·电子结构AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5

    💡 导读：本文建立融合第一性原理电子结构、Wannier低能哈密顿量、Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论和基于Matsubara格林函数的陈数计算的拓扑超导多尺度框架。该框架应用于FeTeSe、单层FeSe及FeSe/GaAs异质结构，预测了由Rashba态、超导配对和轨道杂化共同促进的多种拓扑超导相。

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：对真实材料中拓扑超导性（TSC）的微观理解，需要一种材料信息驱动的方法，在统一的计算框架内整合第一性原理电子结构、超导性和拓扑性。本文通过结合密度泛函理论、基于Wannier的低能哈密顿量、Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论，以及直接在真实多轨道超导哈密顿量上进行的基于Matsubara格林函数的陈数计算，发展了这样一种方法。我们将该框架应用于块体类和单层FeTeSe，以及FeSe/GaAs异质结构，从而能够统一研究本征和近邻诱导的拓扑超导性。我们识别出促进稳健TSC的关键电子结构要素，包括费米能级附近的Rashba活性态、强的诱导超导配对，以及自旋轨道活性部分与超导部分之间显著的轨道杂化。在这些设计原则指导下，我们预测了铁基材料中的多个拓扑超导相，并表明FeSe/GaAs异质结构尤其具有前景，其在实验可及的化学势和低塞曼场下表现出拓扑转变。作为对理论预测的补充，我们展示了FeSe/GaAs异质结构的生长和结构表征，确立了所提出材料平台的实验可行性。我们的结果为直接从真实电子结构设计和筛选候选Majorana材料及异质结构提供了一条定量路径，并为未来面向拓扑超导的材料设计方法奠定了基础。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与我们的超导、拓扑材料、Wannier插值、有效或从头算哈密顿量、界面异质结构和自旋轨道耦合方向高度相关。其将真实材料第一性原理结果直接映射到超导拓扑模型的策略，可借鉴到磁性超导体、二维异质结和拓扑磁性材料设计中。

    启发：建议进一步考察磁序、应变、缺陷和界面无序对拓扑超导相及马约拉纳边界态的影响，并结合自旋哈密顿量或自洽电子-声子耦合。也可阅读其Wannier化与拓扑不变量计算流程，评估在团队现有材料高通量筛选和机器学习哈密顿量中的应用。

    📖 英文原文

    A microscopic understanding of topological superconductivity (TSC) in real materials requires a materials-informed approach that integrates first-principles electronic structure, superconductivity, and topology within a unified computational framework. Here, we develop such an approach by combining density functional theory, Wannier-based low-energy Hamiltonians, Bogoliubov-de Gennes theory, and Matsubara Green's-function-based Chern number calculations performed directly on realistic multiorbital superconducting Hamiltonians. We apply this framework to bulk-like and monolayer FeTeSe and to FeSe/GaAs heterostructures, enabling a unified investigation of both intrinsic and proximity-induced topological superconductivity. We identify key electronic-structure ingredients that promote robust TSC, including Rashba-active states near the Fermi level, strong induced superconducting pairing, and substantial orbital hybridization between spin-orbit-active and superconducting sectors. Guided by these design principles, we predict multiple topological superconducting phases in Fe-based materials and demonstrate that FeSe/GaAs heterostructures are particularly promising, exhibiting topological transitions at experimentally accessible chemical potentials and low Zeeman fields. Complementing the theoretical predictions, we demonstrate the growth and structural characterization of FeSe/GaAs heterostructures, establishing the experimental feasibility of the proposed materials platform. Our results provide a quantitative route for engineering and screening candidate Majorana materials and heterostructures directly from realistic electronic structures and establish a foundation for future materials-by-design approaches to topological superconductivity.

    文献研究：WannierNLQG: A Julia package for nonlinear optical responses and quantum geometry from Wannier tight-binding models#
    WannierNLQG: A Julia package for nonlinear optical responses and quantum geometry from Wannier tight-binding models#
    作者：Zhuocheng Lu、Zhichao Guo、Yuanyuan Xua、Jiacheng Yao、Hua Wang。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5

    💡 导读：该研究介绍了 WannierNLQG Julia 软件包，用于基于 Wannier 紧束缚模型计算非线性光学响应和量子几何性质。软件支持注入流、位移流、自旋响应、贝里曲率、量子度规及简并子空间下的规范一致计算。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与团队的 Wannier 计算、电子结构、非线性光学响应、拓扑材料和自旋相关输运研究高度相关。其对简并能带、量子几何和对称性约束的处理可补充团队现有的电子结构与光学性质计算工具链。

    启发：可评估该软件在二维铁电体、磁性拓扑材料、交替磁性材料和范德华异质结构中的适用性。进一步结合第一性原理和机器学习哈密顿量，可开展应变、层间堆垛或磁序调控下的非线性光学响应研究。

    📖 英文原文

    Nonlinear optical responses and quantum geometry are central to modern condensed matter physics and are deeply intertwined. We introduce WannierNLQG, an extensible Julia framework for computing nonlinear optical responses and quantum geometric quantities from Wannier tight-binding models. Its gauge-consistent, degeneracy-aware architecture accommodates perturbative responses at arbitrary order and additional quantum geometric quantities. The current release evaluates ordinary, spin, and photon-drag injection and shift currents, as Brillouin-zone integrals and on k slices. For shift current, a unified interface exposes four complementary formulations: the conventional method, projector trace, generalized Wilson loop, and a finite-momentum geometric loop, with the last three explicitly accommodating degenerate subspaces. The framework also provides k-resolved Berry curvature, quantum metric, their multipoles, and additional quantum geometric and mixed momentum-spin quantities. Experimental symmetry workflows additionally support symmetry-adapted Wannier construction, symmetrization of tight-binding models and real-space operators, and reduction of spatially uniform response integrals to irreducible k-point orbits and invariant tensor components. The documentation covers formula conventions, the TaskConfig interface, and auditable output formats, with GeS and bilayer MoS2 case studies illustrating method comparison, degeneracy handling, quantum geometric analysis, and photon-drag responses. Together, these capabilities enable first-principles calculations of nonlinear response and quantum geometry in realistic multiband materials, connecting quantum geometry with quantitative materials modeling for optoelectronic, spintronic, and photovoltaic applications.

    四层卡戈梅硼烯中的创纪录元素超导性#
    Record-Breaking Elemental Superconductivity in Tetralayer Kagome Borophene#
    作者：Yingnan Liu、Yan Liu、Renyu Duan、Menghui Wang、Meiling Xu、Hanyu Liu 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 超导AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5

    💡 导读：该工作利用第一性原理计算和全各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，预测四层堆垛kagome硼烯具有约102 K的高临界温度。研究指出层间s-pz成键态与低频面外振动之间的强电子-声子耦合，是其高温超导的主要来源。

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在二维元素晶体中，高于液氮温度的超导性仍然十分罕见，因为强共价键通常能够产生较高的声子频率，却无法提供足够强的电子-声子耦合。本文利用第一性原理计算和完全各向异性的Migdal-Eliashberg理论，预测了由ABAB共价堆垛稳定的四层卡戈梅硼烯，并指出其是一种工作于液氮温度范围的元素超导体。其预测临界温度为102 K，在此前报道的元素超导体中创下最高值。不同于已知的高临界温度含硼超导体主要由面内σ成键态和高频面内B-B伸缩振动主导，四层卡戈梅硼烯实现了一种由面外s-pz成键介导的配对机制，其中费米能级处的层间s-pz成键态与硼原子的低频面外振动发生强耦合。这些结果揭示了多层硼烯中一种独特的面外配对通道，并表明共价堆垛工程可能成为在二维材料中实现高临界温度超导性的途径。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与我们的二维材料、超导、电子-声子耦合和第一性原理计算方向直接相关。其层间堆垛工程和面外振动配对机制可为二维超导材料设计及声子重整化研究提供参考。

    启发：可进一步检验该结构的动力学和热稳定性，并研究应变、载流子掺杂、磁性或衬底效应对超导转变温度的影响。也可结合机器学习势开展大尺度有限温度结构稳定性模拟。

    📖 英文原文

    Superconductivity above the liquid-nitrogen temperature remains rare in two-dimensional elemental crystals, where strong covalent bonding often yields high phonon frequencies but insufficient electron-phonon coupling. Here, using first-principles calculations and fully anisotropic Migdal-Eliashberg theory, we predict tetralayer kagome borophene (TKB) stabilized by ABAB covalent stacking, as a liquid-nitrogen-temperature elemental superconductor. With a predicted critical temperature of 102 K, TKB sets a record-high value among previously reported elemental superconductors. Unlike known high-Tc boron-based superconductors dominated by in-plane sigma-bonding states and high-frequency in-plane B-B stretching modes, TKB realizes an out-of-plane s-pz-bonding-mediated pairing mechanism, in which interlayer s-pz bonding states at the Fermi level are strongly coupled to low-frequency out-of-plane vibrations of boron atoms. These results reveal a distinct out-of-plane pairing channel in multilayer borophene and establish covalent stacking engineering as a potential route for high-Tc superconductivity in two-dimensional materials.

    Na3Bi结构、力学、振动、热学、电子与光学性质的第一性原理研究：一种拓扑狄拉克半金属#
    First-principles investigation of structural, mechanical, vibrational, thermal, electronic and optical properties of Na3Bi: a topological Dirac semimetal#
    作者：Maisha Fahmida、Syed Shovon Mahbub Mahin、Suptajoy Barua、Ishtiaque M. Syed。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 拓扑·电子结构AI×科学交叉2方向匹配6

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在过去几十年中，拓扑材料性质的探索一直是凝聚态物理学中活跃的研究领域。本文利用密度泛函理论，对Na3Bi这一最早发现的拓扑狄拉克半金属之一的结构、弹性力学、热学、电子和光学性质进行了第一性原理研究。研究发现，Na3Bi的六方相在机械和动力学上均稳定，并表现出中等程度的弹性各向异性，其普适各向异性指数为0.832。该化合物的可加工性指数为2.386，属于中等水平；硬度值较低，为2.005，这与其以脆性、共价键合特征为主并伴有轻微金属性贡献的性质相一致。其相对较低的德拜温度为181.39 K，表明该材料晶格较软，原子之间的束缚较弱。电子结构计算证实其具有半金属性，在费米能级处存在定义清晰的狄拉克点，且费米面由点状口袋构成。在宽广的光谱范围内进行的光学分析表明，该材料具有40%至45%的非选择性中等反射率；在可见光区域，其折射率异常高，可达到2至4.5。研究还发现，Na3Bi能够有效吸收紫外辐射。总体而言，这些结果为六方Na3Bi的力学、热学和光学行为建立了全面基线，并指出其在声学阻尼、紫外传感和光电器件应用中的潜在价值。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure calculation、density functional theory。

    📖 英文原文

    Exploring the properties of topological materials has remained an active area of research in condensed matter physics for the past few decades. Here, we present a first-principles investigation of the structural, elasto-mechanical, thermal, electronic, and optical properties of Na3Bi, one of the earliest discovered topological Dirac semimetals, using density functional theory. The hexagonal phase of Na3Bi is found to be both mechanically and dynamically stable, exhibiting moderate elastic anisotropy with a universal anisotropy index of 0.832. The compound displays a moderate machinability index of 2.386 alongside a low hardness value of 2.005, consistent with its predominantly brittle, covalently bonded character accompanied by a slight metallic contribution. Its comparatively low Debye temperature of 181.39K points to a mechanically soft lattice with loosely bound atoms. Electronic structure calculations confirm semimetallic behavior, with well-defined Dirac points pinned at the Fermi level and a Fermi surface composed of point-like pockets. Optical analysis across a wide spectral range reveals non-selective, moderate reflectivity of 40 to 45% and, in the visible region, an unusually high refractive index reaching between 2 and 4.5. Na3Bi is additionally found to be an efficient absorber of ultraviolet radiation. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive baseline for the mechanical, thermal, and optical behavior of hexagonal Na3Bi and point to its potential relevance for acoustic damping, UV-sensing, and optoelectronic applications

    铁-脱氢靛蓝配位聚合物中Haldane链物理的证据#
    Evidence of Haldane-Chain Physics in an Fe-Dehydroindigo Coordination Polymer#
    作者：Ritam Chakraborty、T. V. Ramakrishnan。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：arXiv:2609.06684v1；通告类型：新稿。摘要：我们研究一种铁-脱氢靛蓝自旋交叉配位聚合物在其本征、无衬底极限下是否能够支持Haldane链物理。自旋轨道耦合密度泛函理论计算和磁能映射给出了近乎各向同性的反铁磁耦合J_H = 32.206 meV，同时单离子各向异性要弱得多。利用这些参数，精确对角化和密度矩阵重整化群计算得到一个非简并基态，该基态与最低的三重态衍生激发之间相隔约13–14 meV的能隙，其尺度符合自旋1 Haldane能隙的预期。各向异性仅导致低能模发生很小的劈裂。动态自旋结构因子将最低谱权重定位在反铁磁波矢q = π附近。这些结果将孤立的顺式脱氢靛蓝链识别为一个具有材料特异性的配位聚合物平台，其微观相互作用使其处于与Haldane链物理一致的有能隙区域。

    📖 英文原文

    We investigate whether an Fe--dehydroindigo coordination polymer can support Haldane-chain physics in its intrinsic, substrate-free limit. Spin-orbit-coupled density-functional-theory calculations and magnetic energy mapping yield a nearly isotropic antiferromagnetic coupling, together with much weaker single-ion anisotropy. Using these parameters, exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations give a nondegenerate ground state separated from the lowest triplet-derived excitations by a gap of about 13--14~meV, on the scale expected for the spin-1 Haldane gap. The anisotropy produces only a small splitting of the low-energy modes. The dynamical spin structure factor places the lowest spectral weight near the antiferromagnetic wave vector q=π. These results identify the isolated cis-dehydroindigo chain as a material-specific coordination-polymer platform whose microscopic interactions place it in a gapped regime consistent with Haldane-chain physics.

    文献研究：Superfluidity in active quantum flocks#
    Superfluidity in active quantum flocks#
    作者：Byjesh N. Radhakrishnan、Reyhaneh Khasseh、Thomas Schmidt、Markus Heyl。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 软物质·流体·统计AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    Active quantum matter has very recently emerged at the intersection between bio- and quantum many-body physics, combining the self-organization of living systems with the coherence of the quantum world. Active quantum systems have been shown to exhibit flocking - a collective phenomenon with no precedent in equilibrium quantum physics. In this work we uncover an unexpected layer of quantum order in active quantum flocks: they can become superfluid. We show that, in addition to the symmetry breaking associated with their directed motion, these flocks can also break an additional U(1) symmetry, giving rise to off-diagonal long-range order. For a microscopic model of active hard-core bosons governed by Lindblad dynamics, we derive an effective long-wavelength description of the single-particle density matrix and demonstrate that the flocking phase develops an instability toward off-diagonal long-range order characteristic of superfluid behavior. Our findings reveal active quantum matter as a promising research direction for discovering exotic nonequilibrium phases of quantum matter.

    📖 英文原文

    Kagome metals exhibit a rich interplay of topology, electronic correlations, and lattice dynamics. Recent discoveries of Kagome materials with a flat band near the Fermi level have revealed a variety of correlated electronic phases. However, elucidating their microscopic origin remains challenging, as realistic descriptions require accounting for multiple orbitals and competing interactions on an equal footing. To disentangle correlation effects from material-specific details and identify the essential physics of the flat-band regime, we study the single-orbital Kagome-Hubbard model at flat-band fillings using dynamical mean-field theory. We find strong signatures of flat-band ferromagnetism, consistent with exact and mean-field ground-state results. Moreover, we uncover an unconventional quasi-ordered phase in which a partially filled spin-polarized flat band pinned at the Fermi level gives rise to persistent local spin fluctuations down to zero temperature, in striking contrast to the classical behavior expected for a conventional ferromagnet. Our results demonstrate that these anomalous fluctuations are an intrinsic consequence of the flat-band degeneracy and establish a minimal framework for understanding correlation effects in flat-band Kagome systems.

    SIM-GRAPH：对称相互作用的通用指南#
    SIM-GRAPH: A universal guide to symmetric interactions#
    作者：R. C. Verstraten、C. Morais Smith。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：对称性在物理学的各个领域都发挥着核心作用，从离散晶格上的傅里叶分析到标准模型中基本粒子的分类，均是如此。在有限量子系统中，对称性通常是离散的，例如反射对称性和旋转对称性。本文提出SIM-GRAPH方法，即对称伊辛模型-图约化与投影哈密顿量方法，利用这些对称性高效计算相互作用量子系统的基态可观测量。通过将所有相互作用投影到系统的一个对称子集上，该方法有效减少了格点数目，因此相较于标准精确对角化能够实现指数级加速。此外，本文还介绍了两种扩展，拓宽了该方法的适用性，并允许在计算成本与精度之间进行可控权衡。

    📖 英文原文

    Symmetry plays a central role throughout physics, from Fourier analysis on discrete lattices to the classification of elementary particles in the standard model. In finite quantum systems, symmetries are often discrete, such as reflection and rotational symmetries. Here, we present the SIM-GRAPH method (Symmetric Ising Models - Graph Reduction And Projected Hamiltonians), which uses such symmetries to efficiently calculate ground-state observables of interacting quantum systems. By projecting all interactions onto a symmetric subset of the system, the method effectively reduces the number of sites, thus providing an exponential speedup over standard exact diagonalization. Furthermore, we introduce two extensions that broaden the applicability of the method and allow for a controlled trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

    利用模拟分岔对合金高阶团簇展开哈密顿量进行约束优化#
    Constrained Optimization of Higher-Order Cluster-Expansion Hamiltonians for Alloys Using Simulated Bifurcation#
    作者：Kazuhide Ichikawa、Satoru Ohuchi、Tomoyasu Yokoyama、Takuma Saito、Yoshiki Matsuda。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配3

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：识别基态和低能原子构型是合金设计中的一个核心问题。团簇展开（CE）方法将固定晶格上的构型能量学表示为有效哈密顿量；对于二元合金，高阶CE模型变为多项式Ising哈密顿量。我们以Au-Cu为模型二元合金，将三阶和四阶团簇展开哈密顿量在固定成分约束下表述为带惩罚项的多项式无约束二元优化（PUBO）问题。我们使用SQBM+对这些PUBO问题进行优化，SQBM+是一种基于模拟分岔的求解器，能够直接处理高阶多项式二元目标函数。这种直接的PUBO处理避免了利用辅助变量构建显式二次重构的需求。成分约束通过二次惩罚项施加，其权重由CE目标函数连续松弛的导数系数估计。针对最多2048个原子的体系进行的基准计算表明，SQBM+能够稳健地获得三阶CE模型的低能可行构型，并且对许多四阶实例仍然有效。根据优化构型构建的形成能凸包恢复了CuAu和Cu3Au的有序化趋势，并揭示了非化学计量成分下的有限尺寸效应。这些结果表明，模拟分岔是进行受约束高阶CE优化以搜索合金构型的一条实用途径。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：effective hamiltonian。

    📖 英文原文

    Identifying ground-state and low-energy atomic configurations is a central problem in alloy design. The cluster-expansion (CE) method represents configurational energetics on a fixed lattice as an effective Hamiltonian; for binary alloys, higher-order CE models become polynomial Ising Hamiltonians. Using Au-Cu as a model binary alloy, we formulate cubic and quartic cluster-expansion Hamiltonians as penalty-augmented polynomial unconstrained binary optimization (PUBO) problems under fixed-composition constraints. We optimize these PUBO problems using SQBM+, a simulated-bifurcation-based solver that can treat higher-order polynomial binary objectives directly. This direct PUBO treatment avoids the need to construct an explicit quadratic reformulation with auxiliary variables. Composition constraints are imposed through quadratic penalty terms, whose weights are estimated from derivative coefficients of the continuous relaxation of the CE objective. Benchmark calculations for systems up to 2048 atoms show that SQBM+ robustly obtains low-energy feasible configurations for cubic CE models and remains effective for many quartic instances. Formation-energy convex hulls constructed from the optimized configurations recover the CuAu and Cu3Au ordering trends and reveal finite-size effects at off-stoichiometric compositions. These results demonstrate simulated bifurcation as a practical route to constrained higher-order CE optimization for alloy configuration search.

    文献研究：A Block Tensor Train Burer-Monteiro Framework for Low-Rank Quantum State Tomography#
    A Block Tensor Train Burer-Monteiro Framework for Low-Rank Quantum State Tomography#
    作者：Shakir Showkat Sofi、Charlotte Vermeylen、Fatemeh Mohammadi、Lieven De Lathauwer。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 量子信息·计算AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    Quantum state tomography is a fundamental technique for estimating the state of a quantum system from measured data and plays a crucial role in evaluating the performance of quantum devices. However, standard estimation methods become computationally prohibitive as the system size increases due to the exponential growth of the density matrix, describing a quantum state, with the number of qubits. We propose a low-rank tensor-network framework for mixed-state quantum state tomography based on a block tensor train (Block-TT) factorization. Specifically, the density matrix is represented as the contraction of a Block-TT with its Hermitian transpose, yielding a TT analogue of the Burer-Monteiro factorization. This parameterization guarantees Hermiticity and positive semidefiniteness by construction while compressing the number of optimization variables from exponential to linear in the number of qubits. Building on this representation, we develop single-site and two-site density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithms for estimating quantum states from compressed measurements. The resulting methods operate directly on the compressed parameterization, support adaptive rank refinement, and exploit efficient tensor-network contractions for expectation-value evaluation. The framework is applicable to a broad class of low-rank quantum states, including pure states, nearly pure states, and ground states that admit accurate tensor-network approximations. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate state reconstruction from limited measurements together with substantial reductions in memory requirements and computational cost compared with conventional low-rank tomography methods.

    阅读原文 ↗

    文献研究：A Structural Map of Potential Correlated Electron Molecular Orbital Materials#
    A Structural Map of Potential Correlated Electron Molecular Orbital Materials#
    作者：Md. Rajbanul Akhond、Alexandru B. Georgescu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5

    📖 英文原文

    Correlated electron molecular orbital (CEMO) materials host emergent electronic states built from molecular orbitals localized over clusters of transition metal ions in a solid, yet have historically been discovered sporadically and generally been treated as isolated case studies. Here, we present criteria and tools to classify and analyze these materials, provide a database of identified materials with cluster-specific symmetry analysis, including cluster point groups, effective cluster sublattice dimensionality. Our electronic structure analysis shows that narrow bands in cluster materials can arise either from relatively isolated cluster molecular orbitals or from a mixed mechanism in which cluster localization coexists with frustrated-lattice flat-band topology. We discuss how CEMO properties do not arise from metal clusters in a solid alone: rather they depend on electron count, symmetry, frustration and correlation strength. By integrating cluster classification with flat-band lattice topology and electrochemistry-relevant information, we provide further relevant information to multiple scientific communities. Applying this approach in a high-throughput screen of 34,548 compounds yields potential 2,627 stable or metastable CEMO compounds with isolated cluster motifs. The accompanying open dataset, Cluster Finder software, and interactive web platform enable systematic exploration of cluster-containing electronic solid state materials and establish a starting point for discovering a new class of correlated quantum materials with a wide array of properties.

    文献研究：Nonlinear Topological Orbital Responses of Antiferromagnetic Skyrmions#
    Nonlinear Topological Orbital Responses of Antiferromagnetic Skyrmions#
    作者：Amir N. Zarezad、Arne Brataas、Alireza Qaiumzadeh。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8
    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion、spin-orbit coupling。

    📖 英文原文

    Antiferromagnetic skyrmions evade the skyrmion Hall effect, but compensation suppresses their conventional topological charge Hall signal. We predict a semiclassical nonlinear topological orbital response of an isolated skyrmion in a PT-symmetric hexagonal antiferromagnet without spin-orbit coupling. In the diffusive, weak-emergent-field regime, the spin-dependent emergent Lorentz force reshapes the carrier distribution, generating a local orbital Hall-current correction and a local orbital accumulation, both quadratic in the applied electric field. The current correction requires spin-asymmetric longitudinal scattering, whereas the accumulation survives spin-symmetric scattering. When the spin-diffusion length greatly exceeds the strip width, the current correction persists while the accumulation approaches zero. Both signals are even under electric-field reversal, odd under reversal of the skyrmion topological charge Q, and helicity independent within this model. These symmetries enable rectified detection and distinguish this mechanism from the Q-even quantum-regime topological orbital Hall effect.

    文献研究：Observation of Magnetic-Anisotropy Crossover and High-Temperature Skyrmions in the Dirac Magnet Fe3Ge with a Distorted Kagome Lattice#
    原题：Observation of Magnetic-Anisotropy Crossover and High-Temperature Skyrmions in the Dirac Magnet Fe3Ge with a Distorted Kagome Lattice#
    作者：Yalei Huang、Xiaowei Lv、Bin Li、Chunqiang Xu、Dhanarajagopal Alltrin、Xiaoxuan Ma 等 16 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配6
    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion。

    📖 英文原文

    Topological materials that simultaneously host robust high-temperature skyrmions and nontrivial electronic band structures have attracted tremendous interest owing to their distinctive advantages for both fundamental research and prospective technological applications. Here, we report the observation of robust skyrmions in the Dirac kagome magnet Fe3Ge, which exhibits a high Curie temperature of ~ 650 K. At room temperature, Fe3Ge shows a large intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity of ~ 380 Ω-1cm-1, originating from its nontrivial electronic band topology. Systematic magnetization measurements reveal a spin reorientation transition at ~ 375 K, indicating a crossover from easy-plane to easy-axis magnetic anisotropy. Below the spin reorientation temperature, a large topological Hall effect is observed, arising from microscopic noncoplanar spin structures. Lorentz transmission electron microscopy shows that mesoscopic skyrmions are stabilized in the easy-axis magnetic anisotropy regime and persist over an exceptionally wide temperature window of 375-650 K, far exceeding that of most previously reported skyrmion-hosting materials. These results establish Fe3Ge as a promising platform for exploring diverse topological properties, with strong potential for advancing future high-temperature spintronic applications, ranging from next-generation information storage to logic computing devices.

    文献研究：Two-magnon response from light scattering in altermagnets#
    Two-magnon response from light scattering in altermagnets#
    作者：Shuyi Li、Lexu Zhao、Chunjing Jia。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配5
    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetic materials、magnetism。

    📖 英文原文

    Altermagnetism is a recently established class of magnetic order that combines fully compensated moments with momentum-dependent spin splitting, yet identifying its spectroscopic fingerprints remains an open challenge. In this work, we investigate finite-momentum two-magnon excitations in a two-dimensional d-wave altermagnet by calculating the two-magnon light scattering intensity in different polarization channels. Using linear spin-wave theory and further incorporating the leading 1/S quantum corrections, we demonstrate that the characteristic magnon splitting of altermagnets shifts the upper edge of the two-magnon continuum and the corresponding spectral features to higher energy at boldsymbolX=(π,0) relative to the conventional antiferromagnet, by an amount linear in the exchange anisotropy |δ J₂|, while leaving the response at the Brillouin-zone center unchanged. Although magnon-magnon interactions strongly redistribute spectral weight toward lower energies, the energy scale associated with the high-energy momentum-selective reconstruction remains robust. The interactions additionally generate a pronounced low-energy two-peak structure that has no counterpart within linear spin-wave theory. At boldsymbolK=(π/2,π/2), we show that the interacting two-magnon resonance is twofold degenerate in the conventional antiferromagnetic phase, while a finite altermagnetic exchange anisotropy lifts this degeneracy, producing two peaks whose separation is linear in |δ J₂|. The characteristic energy scales underlying both features are intrinsic to the two-magnon sector rather than to a specific scattering operator. These findings highlight the potential of finite-momentum two-magnon spectroscopy for identifying altermagnetic order in insulating magnets and motivate momentum-resolved resonant inelastic x-ray scattering studies of candidate altermagnetic materials.

    由外延Co/Pt中的晶体学各向异性实现的可重构无场自旋霍尔纳米振荡器#
    Reconfigurable field-free spin Hall nano-oscillators enabled by crystallographic anisotropy in epitaxial Co/Pt#
    作者：Jong-Guk Choi、Avinash Kumar Chaurasiya、Jaimin Kang、Venkatesh Vadde、Peter G. Lim、Roman Khymyn 等 12 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：自旋霍尔纳米振荡器（SHNO）是用于无线通信、神经形态计算和基于振荡器的伊辛机的纳米尺度微波源，但传统器件需要全局磁偏置。本文利用外延Co/Pt中的晶体学各向异性取代这一偏置。生长c轴位于薄膜平面内的六方密排Co，可产生约0.36 T的各向异性场，并使纳米收缩型SHNO能够在无外加磁场条件下于10 GHz以上实现自振荡。工作电流的极性由剩磁方向选择，从而可以对振荡状态进行非易失性重构。微聚焦布里渊光散射测量证实，非线性响应局限于纳米收缩区域。通过光刻控制电流方向与各向异性轴之间的夹角，可以调节激发阈值，并使两个谱支从彼此分离的模式演化为由单一主导谱支构成的状态，这与相互同步相一致。这些结果表明，外延晶体学各向异性可以成为实现可重构无场自旋电子振荡器及振荡器网络的一条途径。

    📖 英文原文

    Spin Hall nano-oscillators (SHNOs) are nanoscale microwave sources for wireless communication, neuromorphic computing and oscillator-based Ising machines, but conventional devices require a global magnetic bias. Here we replace this bias through crystallographic anisotropy in epitaxial Co/Pt. Growth of hcp Co with its c-axis in the film plane produces an anisotropy field of about 0.36 T and enables field-free auto-oscillations above 10 GHz in nanoconstriction SHNOs. The active current polarity is selected by the remanent magnetization, providing nonvolatile reconfiguration of the oscillation state. Micro-focused Brillouin light scattering confirms that the nonlinear response is confined to the nanoconstriction region. Lithographic control of the angle between the current and anisotropy axes tunes the excitation threshold and drives two spectral branches from separated modes to a dominant single branch, consistent with mutual synchronization. These results establish epitaxial crystallographic anisotropy as a route to reconfigurable field-free spintronic oscillators and oscillator networks.

    近场自旋波成像中由磁振子-磁振子干涉产生的表观零动量信号#
    Apparent Zero-Momentum Signals from Magnon--Magnon Interference in Near-Field Spin-Wave Imaging#
    作者：Julien Berthomier、Romain Lebrun、Vincent Cros、Jamal Ben Youssef、Karim Bouzehouane、Abdelmadjid Anane。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：对相干波进行空间分辨测量时，通常会将测量结果解读为底层本征模谱的直接映射。本文表明，在多模自旋波输运的近场成像中，这种解读并不成立。对钇铁石榴石进行扫描氮-空位磁力测量发现，在某些不存在传播自旋波模的频率处，仍然出现明显的表观低波矢信号。通过调节探针与样品之间的距离并对各向异性的近场响应进行建模，研究确定这些信号源于直接激发的Damon-Eshbach波与缺陷或换能器散射的自旋波之间的相干混合：测量信号会获得位于差分波矢k_exc-k_scat处的空间傅里叶分量，即使组成干涉的两列波都携带有限动量，该差分波矢仍可能为零。这些结果表明，相干激发的近场谱是干涉场谱，而不是本征模图谱；同时，这项工作也为识别纳米尺度磁振子器件中的散射过程提供了警示和分析框架。

    📖 英文原文

    Spatially resolved measurements of coherent waves are commonly read as direct maps of the underlying eigenmode spectrum. We show that this interpretation fails in near-field imaging of multimode spin-wave transport. Scanning nitrogen-vacancy magnetometry of yttrium iron garnet reveals pronounced apparent low-wave-vector signals at frequencies for which no propagating spin-wave mode exists. By tuning the probe-sample distance and modeling the anisotropic near-field response, we identify their origin as coherent mixing between directly excited Damon-Eshbach waves and defect- or transducer-scattered spin waves: the measured signal acquires spatial Fourier components at the difference wave vector kₑₓc-kₛcₐₜ, which can vanish even though both constituent waves carry finite momentum. These results establish that near-field spectra of coherent excitations are interferometric field spectra rather than eigenmode maps, providing both a caution and a framework for identifying scattering processes in nanoscale magnonic devices.

    低阻尼亚铁磁薄膜中热磁振子输运的微观理解#
    Microscopic Understanding of Thermal-magnon Transport in a Low-damping Ferrimagnetic Thin Films#
    作者：Lerato Takana、Katya Mikhailova、Junwei Tong、Xiangcheng Liu、Kwangyul Hu、Juan Hofer 等 12 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：热生成的磁振子能够在磁性绝缘体中实现热驱动自旋输运，但控制其传播的微观机制仍未得到充分理解。本文采用将磁振子输运与局域热电效应分离的非局域自旋塞贝克几何构型，研究了低阻尼Li₀.₅Al₁.₀Fe₁.₅O₄/Pt纳米器件中的热磁振子输运。热成像证实探测器区域位于热弛豫长度之外，从而能够进行内禀的非局域测量。我们发现，热磁振子输运会被远高于饱和场的磁场强烈抑制。布里渊光散射表明，增大磁场会降低后向体磁振子的群速度，从而为所观测到的磁振子自旋扩散长度减小提供了微观起源。我们进一步发现，尽管磁振子数目增加，热磁振子输运却随温度升高而减小。只有在纳入温度依赖的交换刚度时，微磁模拟才能复现这一行为。这些结果确定了磁振子群速度和交换刚度是控制亚铁磁薄膜中热磁振子输运的关键参数。

    📖 英文原文

    Thermally generated magnons enable heat-driven spin transport in magnetic insulators, yet the microscopic mechanisms governing their propagation remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate thermal magnon transport in low-damping Li₀.₅Al₁.₀Fe₁.₅O₄/Pt nanodevices using a nonlocal spin Seebeck geometry that separates magnon transport from local thermoelectric effects. Thermal imaging establishes a detector region outside the thermal healing length, enabling intrinsic nonlocal measurements. We find that thermal magnon transport is strongly suppressed by magnetic fields far above saturation. Brillouin light scattering reveals that increasing field reduces the group velocity of backward volume magnons, providing a microscopic origin for the observed reduction in magnon spin diffusion length. We further find that thermal magnon transport decreases with increasing temperature despite an increasing magnon population. Micromagnetic simulations reproduce this behavior only when a temperature-dependent exchange stiffness is included. These results identify magnon group velocity and exchange stiffness as key parameters governing thermal magnon transport in ferrimagnetic thin films.

    螺旋磁性磷族化合物MnP中的半狄拉克态与量子线性磁阻#
    Semi-Dirac States and Quantum Linear Magnetoresistance in Helimagnetic Pnictide MnP#
    作者：Prasanta Chowdhury、Jyotirmoy Sau、Sanat Kumar Adhikari、Sourav Chowdhury、Peter Bencok、Matthias Gutmann 等 10 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：拓扑材料和磁性材料中的巨大线性正磁阻仍是一个受到广泛讨论的问题，尤其是在非共线自旋体系中，自旋依赖散射使电荷输运更加复杂。磷化锰（MnP）是一种具有螺旋磁性的二元磷族化合物，并存在多个由磁场诱导的磁性转变，为研究复杂磁性与电子拓扑之间的相互作用提供了有用平台。本文对高质量MnP单晶中的相依赖磁输运进行了全面的实验和理论研究。霍尔测量表明，在高温下反常霍尔效应主要由偏斜散射主导；在非共线扇形相（FAN）和低温螺旋相（SCR）中，则存在有限的拓扑霍尔效应。在低温下，研究观察到巨大且不饱和的线性正磁阻，在4 K和15 T条件下接近800%；在场极化铁磁相（FM2）中，磁阻表现出显著的线性场依赖关系。第一性原理计算揭示，在Y点存在一个强各向异性的类半狄拉克能带，该能带从SCR相向FAN相和FM2相演化时逐步接近费米能级。我们的分析表明，由此产生的小费米口袋可以在实验可达的磁场下进入极端量子极限，从而依据Abrikosov量子磁阻理论，为所观察到的线性正磁阻提供了微观框架。

    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、magnetic materials、magnetism。

    📖 英文原文

    Large linear positive magnetoresistance (LPMR) in topological and magnetic materials remains a subject of intense debate, particularly in noncollinear spin systems where spin-dependent scattering complicates charge transport. Manganese phosphide (MnP), a helimagnetic binary pnictide with multiple field-induced magnetic transitions, provides a useful platform to investigate the interplay between complex magnetism and electronic topology. Here, we present a comprehensive experimental and theoretical investigation of phase-dependent magnetotransport in high-quality MnP single crystals. Hall measurements reveal an anomalous Hall effect dominated by skew scattering at high temperatures and a finite topological Hall effect in the noncollinear fan (FAN) and low-temperature screw (SCR) phases. At low temperatures, we observe a large, non-saturating LPMR reaching nearly 800 percent at 4 K and 15 T, with a pronounced linear field dependence in the field-polarized ferromagnetic (FM2) state. First-principles calculations reveal a strongly anisotropic semi-Dirac-like band at the Y point that progressively approaches the Fermi level from the SCR to FAN and FM2 states. Our analysis indicates that the resulting small Fermi pocket can access the extreme quantum-limit regime at experimentally accessible fields, providing a microscopic framework for the observed LPMR within Abrikosov's quantum magnetoresistance theory.

    文献研究：Critical temperatures of two dimensional magnets beyond linear spin wave theory: application to CrI₃, MPS₃ (M=Ni, Mn, Fe) and CrSBr#
    Critical temperatures of two dimensional magnets beyond linear spin wave theory: application to CrI₃, MPS₃ (M=Ni, Mn, Fe) and CrSBr#
    作者：Varun Rajeev Pavizhakumari、Thomas Olsen。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配3
    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

    📖 英文原文

    Magnetic anisotropy is crucial for sustaining long range magnetic order in two-dimensional materials (2D) and must be taken into account by any approximate scheme for calculating critical temperatures. While 2D ferromagnets have received significant attention with regard to predicting Curie temperatures, the treatment of 2D anti-ferromagnetism has largely been restricted to classical approaches, which typically underestimate Néel temperatures. The concept of anti-ferromagnetism can be regarded as a special case of single-Q magnetic order, and for such systems the critical temperature can be calculated from the magnon dispersion using either Holstein-Primakoff (HP) bosonization or Green's function-based Random Phase Approximation (RPA). Here, we study the effects of single-ion anisotropy in general single-Q systems in both the HP and RPA methods. In the case of RPA, we generalize the approach to include the Callen Decoupling (CD) correction, which has previously been shown to yield good agreement with experimental Curie temperatures for 2D ferromagnets. We compare the calculated critical temperatures of CrI₃ (uniaxial ferromagnet), MPS₃ (M=Ni, Mn, Fe) (uniaxial anti-ferromagnets) and CrSBr (triaxial ferromagnet) monolayers with experimental values and find that the Green's function-based methods are much more reliable than HP and that the CD decoupling appears to be more accurate than RPA if the single-ion anisotropy is large.

    文献研究：Coherent control of photon pairs via quantum interference between second- and third-order quantum nonlinear processes#
    Coherent control of photon pairs via quantum interference between second- and third-order quantum nonlinear processes#
    作者：Alessia Stefano、Samuel E. Fontaine、J. E. Sipe、Marco Liscidini。arXiv · physics，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    Genuine quantum interference between independent nonlinear processes of different order provides a route to coherent control that cannot be reduced to a classical field interference. Here we present an all-optical analogue of coherent carrier injection by exploiting interference between second- and third-order quantum nonlinear processes in an integrated photonic platform. Photon pairs generated via spontaneous parametric down-conversion and spontaneous four-wave mixing coherently contribute to the same final two-photon state, resulting in a phase-dependent modulation of both the generation rate and the spectral structure of the emitted biphoton state. We illustrate the features of such interference and how it can be used to shape biphoton wavefunctions and their quantum correlations. These results identify interference between nonlinear processes of different order as a distinct form of coherent quantum control within quantum nonlinear optics.

    文献研究：Resolving the ground state intersection problem in coupled cluster theory#
    Resolving the ground state intersection problem in coupled cluster theory#
    作者：Leo Stoll、Federico Rossi、Henrik Koch。arXiv · physics，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配3
    🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

    关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

    📖 英文原文

    A physically correct description of ground state conical intersections is essential for simulating non-adiabatic dynamics of radiationless decay to the electronic ground state. Coupled cluster theory offers a particularly balanced treatment of static and dynamic correlation in excited states. However, coupled cluster methods and other single-reference approaches fail to describe ground state intersection regions, and several workarounds have been proposed to address this shortcoming. Here we present convex similarity constrained coupled cluster theory (CVX-SCC), a framework that resolves the ground state intersection problem by construction. We have applied this theory to two different coupled cluster methods, and tested the resulting models on ethylene, uracil, PSB3, and HeH₂, showing that both variants produce physically correct ground state conical intersections. With future development of nuclear gradients and non-adiabatic coupling vectors, this approach will provide single-reference electronic structures suitable for non-adiabatic dynamics simulations all the way to the ground state.

    文献研究：Effective theory of quantum phases in the dipolar planar rotor chain#
    Effective theory of quantum phases in the dipolar planar rotor chain#
    The Journal of Chemical Physics Current Issue，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 其他AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0
    📖 英文原文

    In this work, we develop a theoretical description of the collective behavior of interacting dipolar planar rotors by using time independent perturbation theory and a small angle quadratic approximation. The ground state properties of both the ordered and disordered quantum phases of the system are directly calculated and analyzed. Time-independent perturbation theory is shown to be appropriate for the disordered phase. For the ordered phase, we construct a quadratic approximation based on the stable equilibrium configurations of the dipolar ordering; we show that the inclusion of the quartic terms from the expansion of the potential energy is essential to correct the shift in the energy spectrum due to quantization ambiguities. Numerical techniques such as exact diagonalization and density matrix renormalization group are used to benchmark the quality of both approximations.

    作者：E. Shirzad。Preprints，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：本文提出了一种精确的解析子域模型，用于考察双定子/转子磁通切换永磁（FSPM）电机的电磁性能；该电机适用于高速透平机械，包括压缩机、鼓风机和涡轮机，亦适用于储能系统，包括飞轮，以及类似应用。研究考虑了三种不同的磁化模式，即切向、径向和Halbach阵列。通过一种依赖于铁芯叠片实际B-H曲线的补偿技术，考虑了磁饱和效应。通过解析求解麦克斯韦方程获得磁场解，并分别推导定子齿和转子齿的饱和系数，以计算非线性运行条件下的气隙磁通密度。利用所提出的模型，比较了三种磁化模式在无轴承应用中的局部牵引力，并计算和比较了所有构型在线性及饱和状态下的气隙磁通密度。对径向和切向磁通分量以及径向压力进行了深入研究。通过与有限元分析（FEA）进行比较，验证了该解析模型的有效性，结果显示二者符合得非常好。

    📖 英文原文

    This paper presents an accurate analytical subdomain model to examine the electromagnetic performance of a dual-stator/rotor flux-switching permanent magnet (FSPM) machine, which is suitable for high-speed turbo machinery (compressors, blowers, turbines), energy storage systems (flywheels), and similar applications. Three different magnetization patterns are considered, namely tangential, radial, and Halbach arrays. Magnetic saturation is taken into account through a compensation technique that relies on the actual B-H curves of the iron laminations. The field solution is obtained by analytically solving Maxwell's equations and the saturation factors for both stator and rotor teeth are derived separately to calculate the air-gap flux density under nonlinear operating conditions. Using the proposed model, the local traction forces are compared across the three magnetization patterns for bearingless applications, and the air-gap flux density is computed and compared across all configurations under both linear and saturated states. The radial and tangential flux components, as well as the radial pressure, are thoroughly investigated. The validity of the analytical model is confirmed through comparisons with finite element analysis (FEA), demonstrating very good agreement.

    作者：Aleksey Lunkin、Yury Holubeu、Denis Golež、Zala Lenarčič。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 超导AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：通过非平衡态工程将超导性稳定在其平衡范围之外，一直是非平衡态调控领域的重要目标。本文报告了与两个热浴耦合的BCS超导体中存在非热配对能隙，其中一个热浴的温度可以远高于平衡临界温度Tc。超导态由一种高度非平衡的稳态维持，该稳态通过与热浴的弱耦合得到稳定，而热浴耦合会打破细致平衡条件。该稳态可以由一个广义吉布斯系综很好地描述，其中的参数是与玻戈留波夫准粒子相对应的化学势。研究发现，对于s波和d波超导体，这种效应都具有强烈的非微扰特征，并讨论了其在基于腔的实验中的可能实现方式。

    📖 英文原文

    Stabilizing superconductivity beyond its equilibrium regime has been one of the quests of nonequilibrium state engineering. We report the presence of nonthermal pairing gap in BCS superconductors coupled to two thermal baths, where one of the baths can be at a temperature well above the equilibrium T_c. Superconducting state is enabled by a highly non-equilibrium steady state stabilized by weak coupling to baths that break detailed balance condition. The steady-state is well described by a generalized Gibbs ensemble parametrized with chemical potentials for associated Bogoliubov quasiparticle. A strong, non-perturbative effect is reported for both s-wave and d-wave superconductors, and its possible realization in cavity-based experiments is discussed.

    光驱动SrTiO3中时间反演对称性的超快控制#
    Light-Driven Ultrafast Control of Time-Reversal Symmetry in SrTiO3#
    作者：In Hyeok Choi、Sergei Urazhdin、Shivasheesh Varshney、Seung Gyo Jeong、Bharat Jalan、Keith A. Nelson。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-09-10
    🧲 物理/材料 · 磁性·自旋电子学AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

    📄 摘要（中文翻译）：光驱动的材料对称性控制能够实现平衡态下被禁止的现象。尽管电磁场已被用于打破时间反演对称性，但在非磁性绝缘氧化物中于超快时间尺度上对其进行动态控制，仍几乎未被探索。本文展示了由失谐椭圆偏振太赫兹脉冲驱动的立方SrTiO3中同时发生的磁对称性降低和时间反演对称性破缺。二次谐波产生（SHG）偏振测量揭示了新出现的SHG圆二色性，这与立方m3m对称性下的三阶非线性过程不一致。SHG偏振图样表现出镜面对称性破缺，表明体系瞬态降低至四方4/mm'm'磁对称性。此外，SHG响应随太赫兹脉冲角动量线性标度变化，为太赫兹场诱导的时间反演对称性破缺提供了直接证据。这些结果为非磁性氧化物中的超快对称性控制建立了直接途径，使得在超快时间尺度上动态操控涌现相成为可能。

    📖 英文原文

    Light-driven control of material symmetry enables the engineering of phenomena forbidden in equilibrium. Although electromagnetic fields have been used to break time-reversal symmetry, its dynamic control on ultrafast timescales in nonmagnetic insulating oxides remains virtually unexplored. Here, we demonstrate simultaneous magnetic symmetry lowering and timereversal-symmetry breaking in cubic SrTiO3 driven by an off-resonant, elliptically polarized THz pulse. Second harmonic generation (SHG) polarimetry reveals emergent SHG circular dichroism inconsistent with third-order nonlinear processes under cubic m3m symmetry. The SHG polar patterns exhibit mirror-symmetry breaking, indicating a transient reduction to tetragonal 4/mm'm' magnetic symmetry. Furthermore, the SHG response scales linearly with the angular momentum of THz pulse, providing direct evidence for THz-field-induced timereversal-symmetry breaking. These results establish a direct pathway for ultrafast symmetry control in non-magnetic oxides, enabling dynamic manipulation of emergent phases on ultrafast timescales.
